Let's design local optimizer variants:

Variant A: Soft-max projected gradient with line search and multiple β.
Pseudo:
def optimize_soft(a0, deadline, max_iters, beta_start, beta_end, lr_start, lr_end, momentum):
    n=len(a); a=clip nonneg sum1; best=a copy; best_score=score.
    S_target=1.
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if deadline exceed stop.
        beta = beta_start * (beta_end/beta_start)^frac or linear.
        C=convolve_fft(a,n,N) (maybe same N)
        p=softmax(beta*C)
        g=gradient_corr(p,a)
        g = g - g.mean()
        a_new = a - lr*g (with momentum maybe)

- `_next_pow2(x)` returns int(round(math.log2(x)))? Use bit ops:

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p*=2
    return p
```

- `_nonneg_sum1(a)`:
```
def _sum1(a):
    a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float),0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-14: return None
    return a/s
```
But original previous normalize target sqrt(2n). Since objective scale invariant, sum1 simpler.

Pseudo:
```
def _push_gradient(a, deadline, iters, beta0, beta1, lr0, lr1, momentum=0.6, score_interval=5):
    n=len(a); a=_sum1(a); N_grad=_next_pow2(3*n-2); N_conv=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=_score(a)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    # Maybe maintain C and p? Each iter compute C, p, g.
    for it in range(iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       frac=it/max(1,iters-1)
       beta=beta0*(beta1/beta0)**frac
       lr=lr0 + (lr1-lr0)*frac maybe
       C=_conv(a,N_conv)
       p=_peaks(C,beta)
       g=_grad(a,p,N_grad)  # returns derivative
       # Center tangent: remove weighted mean? For Euclidean fixed sum, remove unweighted mean (1/n) to keep sum approx. But if using momentum, after projection.
       gc = g - (g.sum()/n)
       v = momentum*v + gc
       # adaptive step: maybe use lr/(norm?)
       a_try = a - lr*v
       a_try=np.maximum(a_try,0)
       s=a_try.sum()
       if s<1e-12: a_try=a
       a_try=a_try/s
       sc=_score_fast(a_try) # use C? Need conv again. Could use _score(a_try)
       if not finite or sc>best_score*(1+1e-6): # maybe accept if sc<current? Ensure descent.
           # try smaller
           accepted=False
           for factor in [0.5,0.25,0.125]:
              at=a - lr*factor*v; ... sc
              if sc < best_score - 1e-12: accept
           if not accepted:
              # keep a, maybe decay lr or perturb
       if accepted:
          a=at; if sc<best_score: best...
       else:
          # small random perturb? maybe not
    return best,best_score
```
This might be slow: 2 conv per iter. Use `_score(a)` computes conv. We can compute C after candidates via N_conv. That's okay for n≤2048, maybe 1000 iter ~? Each conv FFT length up to 8192 for n=2048; 2k FFTs ~ maybe 1ms each -> seconds. Good.

But line search factors many; can limit. Use score interval? We can just update each iter with accepted factors.

Implement `_peaks_from_C(C, focus)`:
```
mx=C.max(); if mx<=0 return uniform
X=(C-mx)/mx  # <=0
Z=np.exp(focus*X)
s=Z.sum(); if s==0: onehot; return Z/s
```
This is stable, avoids overflow. If focus large and all but max underflow, onehot. Good.

```
def _lp_step(a, deadline, attempt=1):

       A[m,lo:hi+1] = a[hi-lo? wait]
```

```
def _spectral_target(a, target_type, params):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L)
   Ctarget=np.zeros(N)
   tparam...
   Ctarget[:L]=...
   F=np.fft.fft(Ctarget)
   B=np.sqrt(F)  # principal
   a2=np.fft.ifft(B).real[:n]
   a2=np.maximum(a2,0)
   if sum>0 a2/=sum...
   return a2
```

```
def _root_sequence(n):
    p=np.zeros(n); p[0]=1.0
    for i in range(n-1): p[i+1]=p[i]*(2*i+1)/(2*i+2)
    return p
```

Linear programming direction top-level:
```
def _lp_dir_solve(a, U, eps):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    A=np.zeros((L,n))
    for m in range(L):
        lo=max(0,m-n+1); hi=min(n-1,m)
        cnt=hi-lo+1
        if lo==0: A[m,0:hi+1]=a[hi::-1]
        else: A[m,lo:hi+1]=a[hi:lo-1:-1]
    c = -np.ones(n)
    C=_conv(a, None) # N maybe not necessary? But conv for score; use raw `np.convolve` maybe for L small? Use FFT.
    M=C.max()
    slack=np.maximum(M - C, 0.0)*0.5
    lower=-a.copy()
    upper=U*a + eps
    # If slack all negative? M-C >=0; at max slack0.
    # Add slack tiny? To avoid infeasibility due roundoff: slack += 1e-12*M
    res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=slack,bounds=list(zip(lower,upper)),method='highs',options={'time_limit':120,'presolve':True}) maybe time_limit to avoid stall. But if time_limit exceeded, res? We can just let; monitor deadline outside.
    if res.status in (0,2? optimal after timeout?) high=2? linprog status: 0 optimal, 1 fail, 2 unbounded, 3 infeasible, 4 numerical. method high may return 2 if max iteration; but x may feasible? Use status 0 only.
    return res.x, M, C
```
Bounds list length up to 2048; okay. `options` can include `'time_limit': 60` maybe. But if time limit causes status 2, we ignore. We should use total deadline: set time_limit = max(5, deadline - now) - but linprog options time_limit expects seconds. We can pass 120. If too long, may stop; okay. We can call multiple.

```
def _optimize_lp_dir(a, deadline, max_iters=50, U=1.0, min_sum_improve=1e-8):
   a=np.maximum(a,0); # no sum1? scale? We can normalize sum1 to keep consistent.
   a=_sum1(a)
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
      u,M,C = _lp_dir_solve(a, U, 1e-8)
      if u is None: break
      sum_u = u.sum()
      if sum_u <= 1e-10*max(1,abs(np.sum(a))):
         # try larger U or smaller? maybe break or random perturb
         # Could try U=2 once
         continue
      # line search
      best_local, sc = _line_search_dir(a,u,1.0)
      if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          a=best_local; best=...; continue with smaller U? 
      else:
          # Maybe try u scaled? If sum positive but direction fails due nonlinear, try smaller; line search already.
          # Use U=2 or random.
          # break if no improve.
          if it>5: break
   return best,best_sc
```

```
def _grad_polish(a, deadline, focus_start=5.0, focus_end=200.0, lr_start=0.35, lr_end=0.05, momentum=0.5, max_iters=500, accept_window=1e-10):
```

Pseudo detailed:
```
def _grad_polish(a, deadline, focus0, focus1, lr0, lr1, momentum, max_iters, allowed=True):
    n=len(a)
    a=_sum1(a)
    if a is None: return a,float('inf')
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    # precompute Nconv, Ngrad
    Nconv=_next_pow2(2*n-1); Ngrad=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       frac=it/max(1,max_ffiters-1)
       focus=focus0 + (focus1-focus0)*frac
       lr=lr0 + (lr1-lr0)*frac
       C=_conv(a,Nconv)
       p=_peaks(C,focus)
       g=_grad(p,a,Ngrad)
       g -= g.mean()
       v = momentum*v + g
       direction = -v
       scale = np.max(np.abs(direction))
       if scale <= 1e-15: break
       direction /= scale
       # try steps
       accepted=False
       for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
           step = lr*factor
           a_try = a + step*direction
           if np.any(a_try < -1e-8):
               a_try = np.maximum(a_try,0.0)
           s=a_try.sum()
           if s<1e-12: continue
           a_try /= s
           sc=_score_fast? need conv. Use _score(a_try) maybe heavy.
           if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best=a_try.copy(); best_sc=sc; a=a_try; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           # Try pure gradient no momentum?
           d=-g; scale; a_try = np.maximum(a+0.1*lr*d/scale,0) ...
       # occasionally print? Maybe every 100? Can't print too much.
    return best,best_sc
```
This line search using best_sc only accepts improvements over global best, not current. That is stochastic hill-climbing; may accept plateau? We want local descent, but beta/focus changes may make objective nonmonotonic; accepting only best may reduce progress. We can accept if sc < current_sc (not best). But need current_sc computed. So use current_sc for line search. Track best separately. This allows progress.

```
current_sc = 2*n*Cmax
```

```
def _modulated_root_starts(n, rng, num=30):
    p=_root_sequence(n); i=np.arange(n)
    for _ in range(num):
       f=rng.uniform(0.05,0.9)  # cycles per length? cos 2π f i/n
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       d=rng.uniform(-0.7,0.7)
       q=p*(1+d*np.cos(2π*f*i/n+ph))
       q=np.maximum(q,1e-8)
       yield q
```

Implementation details:

`_score_sequence`:
```
def _score_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(a>0,a,0.0)  # handle nan? If nan, max? Need finite check.
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return inf
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    S=a.sum()
    if S < 1e-12: return inf
    N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.fft(a,N)
    C=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
    maxC=C.max()
    if maxC < 0 and maxC > -1e-15: maxC=0.0
    if maxC < 0: return inf? impossible
    return float(2.0*n*maxC/(S*S))
```
If sequence has elements too large? eval clamps min1000 but output okay. We keep small.

`_normalize_sum1`:
```
def _sum1(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0 or not allfinite: return None
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    a=np.minimum(a,1e6) maybe
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-14: return (np.ones(a.size)/a.size)
    return a/S
```
`np.isfinite` check.

`_convolution(a,N=None)`:
```
def _convolution(a, N=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size; L=2*n-1; if N is None: N=_next_pow2(L)
   F=np.fft.fft(a,N); C=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:L]
   return C
```

`_peak_weights(C, focus)`:
```
def _peak_weights(C, focus):
    C=np.asarray(C,float)
    mx=float(C.max())
    L=C.size
    if mx <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(mx):
       return np.full(L,1.0/L)
    X=(C-mx)/mx
    z=np.clip(focus*X, -700, 0)
    w=np.exp(z)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w=np.zeros(L); w[int(C.argmax())]=1.0
    else:
       w/=s
    return w
```

`_gradient_from_weights(a,p,N=None)`:
```
def _gradient_from_weights(a,p,N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    Fa=np.fft.fft(a[::-1],N)
    Fp=np.fft.fft(p,N)
    full=np.fft.ifft(Fp*Fa).real[:L+n-1]
    g=full[n-1:n-1+n]
    return 2.0*g
```
Need ensure full index length: L+n-1=2n-1+n-1=3n-2. Good. If n=1, L=1, N? _next_pow2(1)=1? FFT length 1 okay; p len1; rev len1; full length1; full[0:1].

`_grad_polish`:
Detailed implementation with no nested functions:
```
def _grad_polish(a, deadline, focus0=5.0, focus1=100.0, lr0=0.35, lr1=0.05, momentum=0.45, max_iters=400, name=""):
    a=_sum1(a)
    if a is None: return a,inf
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_sequence(a)
    current_sc=best_sc
    Nconv=_next_pow2(2*n-1); Ngrad=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    decay=1.0
    no_improve=0
    last_print=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       frac = it / max(1, max_iters)
       focus = focus0 + (focus1-focus0)*frac
       lr = lr0 + (lr1-lr0)*frac
       C = _convolution(a, Nconv)
       mx=float(C.max())
       if mx <= 0: current_sc = 0? else current_sc=2*n*mx # since sum1
       if not finite or mx<=0: break
       p = _peak_weights(C, focus)
       g = _gradient_from_weights(a,p,Ngrad)
       g = g - g.mean()
       v = momentum*v + g
       direction = -v
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(direction)))
       if scale <= 1e-15: break
       direction /= scale
       accepted=False
       factors = (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125)
       for f in factors:
           if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
           step = lr*decay*f
           at = a + step*direction
           at = np.maximum(at,0.0)
           s=at.sum()
           if s <= 1e-14: continue
           at = at/s
           sc = _score_sequence(at)
           if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
           if sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
               a=at; current_sc=sc; accepted=True
               if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                   best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
               no_improve=0
               # optionally decay reset?
               decay = min(1.0, decay*1.05)
               break
       if not accepted:
           no_improve += 1
           decay *= 0.7
           if decay < 0.03:
               # small noise? break
               break
       if no_improve > 80 and it % 20 == 0:
           # random perturb and score? Could break.
           pass
    if best_sc is inf? 
    return best,best_sc
```
But note `current_sc=2n*mx` because sum1. We should recompute `C` for current before line search; after no accepted, current_sc same. If accepted, current_sc sc.
The line search compares to current_sc; if focus changes, current same. Good.
Potential issue: `_score_sequence` recomputes conv, but C_try may be similar. Could optimize by computing C_try via `_convolution(at,Nconv)` and `2*n*C_try.max()`. For sum1 after normalization; yes. def _score_normalized(a,N):
```
   if N is None: N=...
   C=_convolution(a,N); mx=C.max(); return 2*a.size*mx if mx>0 else 2*a.size
```

```
def _score_norm_n(a,n,N):

    M=float(C.max())
    if M<=1e-15: return 2*n * max(0,M)
    return 2*n*M
```

`_lp_direction`:
```
def _build_A(a):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; A=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(L):
        lo=max(0,m-n+1); hi=min(n-1,m)
        if lo==0:
            A[m,0:hi+1] = a[hi::-1]
        else:
            A[m,lo:hi+1] = a[hi:lo-1:-1]
    return A
```

```
def _lp_direction(a, upper_factor=1.0, eps=1e-8, timeout=60):
    n=a.size; A=_build_A(a)
    C=_convolution(a)
    M=float(C.max())
    if not finite or M<=0: return None

    # add tiny to active?: slack += 1e-12*M
    lower=-a.copy()

    c=-np.ones(n)
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=slack, bounds=list(zip(lower,upper)), method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'time_limit':max(1,int(timeout))})
    if res is None or not hasattr(res,'x') or res.x is None: return None
    x=np.asarray(res.x,float)
    if res.status not in (0,2):
```

```
Line search:
```
def _line_search_dir(a,u, deadline):
    best=a; best_sc=_score_sequence(a)
    # Compute alpha max
    alpha_max=1e6
    neg = u < -1e-14
    if np.any(neg):
        alpha_max = float(np.min(-a[neg]/u[neg]) * 0.999)
        if alpha_max <= 0: alpha_max=0.0
    alphas=[]
    for val in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0,4.0):
       if val <= alpha_max: alphas.append(val)
    if not alphas:
       if alpha_max >1e-4: alphas.append(alpha_max*0.5)
    # ensure at least 0.1 if allowed
    for al in alphas:
       at=a+al*u; at=np.maximum(at,0); s=at.sum(); if s<=0: continue; at/=s; sc=_score_sequence(at); if sc<best_sc: update
    return best,best_sc

def _lp_dir_optimize(a, deadline, max_iters=20, upper_factor=1.0, timeout=120):
    a=_sum1(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_norm(a)
    n=a.size; no=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
       u=_lp_direction(a, upper_factor, timeout=max(5, deadline-time.monotonic()-15))
       if u is None:
           # try smaller upper
           if upper_factor > 0.2:
              upper_factor *= 0.5; continue
           break
       # line search
       a2, sc = _line_search_dir(a,u, deadline)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best=a2.copy(); best_sc=sc; a=a2; no=0; continue
       else:
           no +=1
           if upper_factor>0.2: upper_factor *=0.5
           if no>6: break
    return best,best_sc
```

Now, candidate start generation:
```
def _root_sequence(n):
    p=np.zeros(n, dtype=float); p[0]=1.0
    for i in range(n-1):
       p[i+1] = p[i] * (2.0*i + 1.0) / (2.0*i + 2.0)
    return p
```
For n up to 4096, recurrence stable; values ~1/√n ~0.016.

`_modulate_root(n, rng, idx)`:
```
def _modulated_root(n, rng, idx):
    p=_root_sequence(n)
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    f = rng.uniform(0.03, 0.8)
    ph = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
    d = rng.uniform(-0.8,0.8)
    # Sometimes use two modes
    mode = int(rng.integers(0,3))
    if mode ==0:
       w=1+d*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*i/n + ph)
    elif mode==1:
       w=1+d*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*i/n + ph) + 0.5*d*np.cos(2*np.pi*(2*f)*i/n - ph)
    else:
       w=1+d*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*i/n + ph)
    q=p*w
    q=np.where(q>0,q,1e-8)
    return q
```
Need no lambda. Use `int(rng.integers(0,3))` okay.

Also maybe use "root_taper": p * (0.5+0.5*cos(π i/(n-1)))? This lowers endpoint; can evaluate. We can generate variations:
```
def _tapered_root(n):
    p=_root_sequence(n); i=np.arange(n); w=0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*i/(n-1)) # 1 to 0; to avoid zero endpoints? w=0.2+0.8*cos?

   b=(a0+a0[::-1])*0.5; b=_sum1(b); if b None return a0,_score_sequence(a0)

def _random_perturb_check(a, rng, num=10, scales=(0.03,0.08,0.15)):
    a=_sum1(a); if a is None: return a,inf
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_sequence(a)
    n=a.size; scale_base=max(float(a.max()), 1e-6)
    N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    for s in scales:

          noise=s*scale_base*rng.standard_normal(n)
          b=a+noise; b=np.maximum(b,0.0); bb=_sum1(b); if bb None: continue
          C=_convolution(bb,N); mx=float(C.max())
          sc=2*n*mx if mx>0 else 2*n
          if sc<best_sc*0.999? Use <best_sc-1e-12.
    return best,best_sc
```

Pseudo code for `_process_sequence`:
```
def _process_sequence(a, deadline, do_lp=True, max_lp_iters=15, lp_timeout=120, name="seq", seed=42):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    a0=_sum1(a)
    if a0 is None: return a0, inf
    best=a0.copy(); best_sc=_score_sequence(a0)
    # sym
    b,bs=_try_symmetrize(a0)
    if bs < best_sc:
       best=b.copy(); best_sc=bs; a0=b
    total = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if total < 30: return best,best_sc
    # split
    if do_lp and len(a0) <= 2048:
       grad_fraction = 0.45 if do_lp else 0.8
    else:
       grad_fraction = 0.9
    grad_deadline = t_start + grad_fraction*total
    lp_deadline = max(grad_deadline, deadline - 30.0)
    # But if do_lp false, lp_deadline=t_start.
    # settings
    if n <=512: settings list as above
    grad_time = max(0.0, grad_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if grad_time > 5:
       per = max(15.0, grad_time / len(settings))
       for s in settings:
           sub_dead = min(grad_deadline, time.monotonic() + per)
           if time.monotonic() > sub_dead - 2: break
           seq, sc = _grad_polish(best, sub_dead, s[0],...)
           if sc < best_sc: update; best=seq
    # LP
    if do_lp and n <= 2048 and time.monotonic() < lp_deadline - 15:
       # If n>1024 reduce max LP
       seq, sc = _lp_dir_optimize(best, lp_deadline, max_iters=max_lp_iters, timeout=max(10, min(lp_timeout, lp_deadline-time.monotonic()-10)))
       if sc<best_sc: update
    # final gradient
    fin_dead = deadline - 5.0
    if time.monotonic() < fin_dead - 20:
       seq, sc = _grad_polish(best, fin_dead, 30,300,0.25,0.05,0.5,200)
       if sc<best_sc: update
    # random perturb
    if time.monotonic() < fin_dead - 10:
       rngp=np.random.default_rng(seed + int(n))
       seq, sc = _random_perturb_check(best, rngp, num=8, scales=(0.03,0.07))
       if sc<best_sc: update
    # final sym
    b,bs=_try_symmetrize(best)
    if bs<best_sc: update
    return best,best_sc

`_vertex_direction` code:
```
def _vertex_direction(a, g, U=1.0):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    target=1.0/(U+1.0)
    d=-a.copy()
    pos_mass=0.0
    order=np.argsort(g)
    for idx in order:
        if pos_mass >= target: break
        ai=float(a[idx])
        if ai <= 1e-15:
            # if zero, skip
            continue
        rem=target-pos_mass
        if rem >= ai:
            d[idx] = U*ai
            pos_mass += ai
        else:
            gamma=rem/ai if ai>0 else 0.0
            d[idx] = -ai + gamma*(U+1.0)*ai
            pos_mass = target
            break
    s=d.sum()
    if abs(s) > 1e-12:
        d -= s/n
    return d
```
Since target fraction of total mass 1/(U+1), if a sum1. If a not sum1 (we normalize), but can normalize target based on total mass? If sum not exactly1, use total = a.sum(), target=total/(U+1), and d initial -a. Let's pass after sum1; still use total.
```
total=sum(a); target=total/(U+1)
pos_mass += ai; etc.
```

Detailed code:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    p = 1
    m = int(max(1, m))
    while p < m: p <<= 1
    return p
```

`_finite_nonneg`:
```
def _finite_nonneg(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   if a.size==0: return None
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return None
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   return a
```

`_sum1`:
```
def _sum1(a):
   a=_finite_nonneg(a)
   if a is None: return None
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s <= 1e-14:
      return np.ones(a.size,dtype=float)/a.size
   return a/s
```

`_score_sequence` uses `_sum1`? No, uses a not normalized. We call `_finite_nonneg`, sum. If sum small return inf. `N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)`. C. Return.

```
full = np.fft.ifft(Fp*Fa)
full = full.real[:L+n-1]
g = full[n-1:n-1+n]

```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: b=float(budget_s)
    except: b=2350
    if b < 100: b=2350
    use = min(2380.0, max(b, 2350.0))
    deadline = t0 + use
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_seq=None; best_score=inf
    tasks=[]
    # global
    try:
       gs=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=np.float64)
       gs=_finite_nonneg(gs)
       if gs is not None and gs.size >= 16:
          if gs.size > 4096: gs=gs[:4096]
          tasks.append((gs.copy(), "existing", 900, True))
          for L in (512,1024):
             if gs.size >= L:
                tasks.append((gs[:L].copy(), f"existing_{L}", 500, True))
    except Exception:
       pass
    # roots
    tasks.append((_root_sequence(512), "root512", 900, True))
    tasks.append((_root_sequence(1024), "root1024", 700, True))
    for k in range(3):
       tasks.append((_modulated_root(512, rng, k), f"mod512_{k}", 220, True))
    for k in range(2):
       tasks.append((_modulated_root(1024, rng, k), f"mod1024_{k}", 220, False)) # maybe no LP for mod1024? Could True but heavy. Let's True limited.
    tasks.append((_root_sequence(2048), "root2048", 300, False))
```

```python
"""
Searches for non-negative coefficient sequences minimizing
2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)^2).

Strategy:
- Use polynomial/convolution structure and root-like starts.
- Use FFT-based convolutions and smooth max (softmax) gradients.
- Use a bounded steepest-descent direction in the simplex.
- Use a local LP direction that increases total mass while keeping the
  peak convolution approximately fixed.
- Run several lengths and restarts within the time budget.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linprog


def _next_pow2(m):
    p = 1
    m = int(max(1, m))
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _finite_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return a


def _sum1(a):
    a = _finite_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def _score_sequence(a):
    a = _finite_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(a.size)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.fft(a, N)
    C = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:2 * n - 1]
    mx = float(C.max())
    if mx < 0.0 and mx > -1e-12:
        mx = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx < 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def _convolution(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(L)
    F = np.fft.fft(a, N)
    C = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
    return C


def _peak_weights(C, focus):
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(C.size)
    mx = float(C.max())
    if mx <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / max(1, L), dtype=np.float64)

    X = (C - mx) / mx
    z = np.clip(focus * X, -700.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(C.argmax())] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def _gradient_from_weights(a, p, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    Fp = np.fft.fft(p, N)
    Fa = np.fft.fft(a[::-1], N)
    full = np.fft.ifft(Fp * Fa).real[:L + n - 1]
    g = full[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    return 2.0 * g


def _vertex_direction(a, g, U=1.0):
    """Steepest bounded feasible direction for min g.d, sum(d)=0, -a<=d<=U*a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    total = float(a.sum())
    if total <= 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    target = total / (U + 1.0)
    d = -a.copy()
    pos_mass = 0.0
    order = np.argsort(g, kind="mergesort")

    for idx in order:
        if pos_mass >= target:
            break
        ai = float(a[idx])
        if ai <= 1e-18:
            continue
        rem = target - pos_mass
        if rem >= ai:
            d[idx] = U * ai
            pos_mass += ai
        else:
            if rem <= 1e-15:
                d[idx] = -ai
            else:
                gamma = rem / ai if ai > 0.0 else 0.0
                d[idx] = -ai + gamma * (U + 1.0) * ai
            pos_mass = target
            break

    s = float(d.sum())
    if abs(s) > 1e-12:
        d -= s / n
    return d


def _grad_polish(a, deadline, focus0=5.0, focus1=100.0, lr0=0.8, lr1=0.1,
                 momentum=0.3, max_iters=600, U=1.0):
    a = _sum1(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    n = int(a.size)
    amax = float(a.max())
    floor = max(1e-15, 1e-10 * amax)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = _sum1(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_sequence(best)

    Nconv = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Ngrad = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    decay = 1.0
    no_improve = 0

    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        frac = float(it) / max(1, max_iters)
        focus = focus0 + (focus1 - focus0) * frac
        lr = lr0 + (lr1 - lr0) * frac

        C = _convolution(a, Nconv)
        mx = float(C.max())
        if mx < 0.0 and mx > -1e-12:
            mx = 0.0
        if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
            break

        current_sc = float(2.0 * n * mx)

        p = _peak_weights(C, focus)
        g = _gradient_from_weights(a, p, Ngrad)
        g = g - float(g.mean())

        v = momentum * v + (1.0 - momentum) * g
        d = _vertex_direction(a, v, U=U)
        ds = float(d.sum())
        if abs(ds) > 1e-12:
            d -= ds / n

        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if scale <= 1e-15:
            break

        accepted = False
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            step = lr * decay * factor
            at = a + step * d
            at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
            st = float(at.sum())
            if st <= 1e-14:
                continue
            at = at / st

            Ct = _convolution(at, Nconv)
            mt = float(Ct.max())
            if mt < 0.0 and mt > -1e-12:
                mt = 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(mt):
                continue
            sc = float(2.0 * n * mt)

            if sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
                a = at
                current_sc = sc
                accepted = True
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best = at.copy()
                    best_sc = sc
                no_improve = 0
                decay = min(1.0, decay * 1.05)
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            decay *= 0.72
            if no_improve > 120 or decay < 0.02:
                break

    return best, best_sc


def _build_conv_matrix(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(L):
        lo = max(0, m - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, m)
        if lo == 0:
            A[m, 0:hi + 1] = a[hi::-1]
        else:
            A[m, lo:hi + 1] = a[hi:lo - 1:-1]
    return A


def _lp_direction(a, upper_factor=1.0, timeout=60.0):
    """
    Linearized direction:
      max sum(u)
      s.t. conv(a,u)_t <= (M - c_t(a))/2,
           -a_i <= u_i <= upper_factor*a_i + eps.
    This increases total mass while approximately keeping the peak fixed.
    """
    a = _sum1(a)
    if a is None:
        return None

    n = int(a.size)
    A = _build_conv_matrix(a)
    C = _convolution(a)
    M = float(C.max())
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    slack = 0.5 * np.maximum(M - C, 0.0)
    slack += 1e-12 * max(1.0, M)

    lower = -a.copy()
    eps = max(1e-12, 1e-8 * float(a.max()))
    upper = upper_factor * a + eps
    cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        opts = {
            "presolve": True,
            "time_limit": int(max(1, min(240, timeout)))
        }
        res = linprog(
            cobj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=slack,
            bounds=list(zip(lower, upper)),
            method="highs",
            options=opts
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "status", None) != 0:
        return None
    if not hasattr(res, "x") or res.x is None:
        return None

    u = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return None
    u = np.clip(u, lower, upper)
    su = float(u.sum())
    if su <= 1e-10:
        return None
    return u


def _line_search_dir(a, u, deadline):
    base_sc = _score_sequence(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = base_sc

    if not np.isfinite(base_sc):
        return best, best_sc

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)

    alpha_max = 1e6
    neg = u < -1e-14
    if np.any(neg):
        if np.any(u[neg] == 0):
            return best, best_sc
        alpha_max = float(np.min(-a[neg] / u[neg])) * 0.999
        if alpha_max <= 0.0:
            return best, best_sc

    candidates = []
    for cc in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 4.0):
        if cc <= alpha_max + 1e-12:
            candidates.append(cc)
    if alpha_max > 0.0:
        candidates.append(0.5 * alpha_max)

    for al in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        at = a + al * u
        at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
        st = float(at.sum())
        if st <= 1e-14:
            continue
        at = at / st
        sc = _score_sequence(at)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = at.copy()
            best_sc = sc

    return best, best_sc


def _lp_dir_optimize(a, deadline, max_iters=15, timeout_max=120.0):
    a = _sum1(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_sequence(best)

    U_values = (1.0, 2.0, 0.5)
    for U in U_values:
        bad = 0
        for _ in range(max(1, max_iters)):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 25.0:
                break

            timeout = max(5.0, min(float(timeout_max), rem - 12.0))
            u = _lp_direction(a, upper_factor=U, timeout=timeout)
            if u is None:
                break

            a2, sc2 = _line_search_dir(a, u, deadline)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-11:
                best = a2.copy()
                best_sc = sc2
                a = a2
                bad = 0
            else:
                bad += 1
                if bad >= 3:
                    break

    return best, best_sc


def _root_sequence(n):
    n = int(n)
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return p
    p[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(n - 1):
        p[i + 1] = p[i] * (2.0 * i + 1.0) / (2.0 * i + 2.0)
    return p


def _modulated_root(n, rng, idx=0):
    p = _root_sequence(n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.8))
    ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    d = float(rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8))
    m = int(rng.integers(0, 3))

    if m == 0:
        w = 1.0 + d * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * i / max(1, n) + ph)
    elif m == 1:
        w = 1.0 + d * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * i / max(1, n) + ph)
        w += 0.5 * d * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (2.0 * f) * i / max(1, n) - ph)
    else:
        w = 1.0 + d * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * i / max(1, n) + ph)

    q = p * w
    q = np.where(q > 1e-12, q, 1e-10)
    return q


def _try_symmetrize(a):
    a0 = _sum1(a)
    if a0 is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    sa = _score_sequence(a0)
    b = 0.5 * (a0 + a0[::-1])
    b = _sum1(b)
    if b is None:
        return a0, sa
    sb = _score_sequence(b)
    if np.isfinite(sb) and sb < sa:
        return b, sb
    return a0, sa


def _random_perturb_check(a, rng, num=6, scales=(0.03, 0.07)):
    a0 = _sum1(a)
    if a0 is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    best = a0.copy()
    best_sc = _score_sequence(a0)
    n = int(a0.size)
    scale_base = max(1e-12, float(a0.max()))
    N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    for s in scales:
        for _ in range(num):
            b = a0 + (s * scale_base) * rng.standard_normal(n)
            b = _sum1(b)
            if b is None:
                continue
            C = _convolution(b, N)
            mx = float(C.max())
            if mx < 0.0 and mx > -1e-12:
                mx = 0.0
            sc = float(2.0 * n * mx)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = b.copy()
                best_sc = sc

    return best, best_sc


def _process_sequence(a, deadline, do_lp=True, max_lp_iters=12,
                      lp_timeout=120.0, name="seq", seed=42):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    a0 = _sum1(a)
    if a0 is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    best = a0.copy()
    best_sc = _score_sequence(best)

    sym, sym_sc = _try_symmetrize(a0)
    if sym is not None and np.isfinite(sym_sc) and sym_sc < best_sc:
        best = sym.copy()
        best_sc = sym_sc
        a0 = sym

    n = int(best.size)
    if do_lp and n > 1600:
        do_lp = False

    print(f"[{name}] start n={n} score={best_sc:.6f}")

    total = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if total < 15.0:
        return best, best_sc

    if do_lp:
        grad_end = t_start + 0.42 * total
        lp_end = deadline - 30.0
    else:
        grad_end = deadline - 30.0
        lp_end = t_start

    if n <= 512:
        settings = [
            (5.0, 60.0, 0.80, 0.15, 0.25, 900),
            (20.0, 400.0, 0.55, 0.10, 0.35, 650),
            (120.0, 2500.0, 0.30, 0.05, 0.45, 500),
        ]
    elif n <= 1024:
        settings = [
            (5.0, 60.0, 0.70, 0.12, 0.25, 600),
            (20.0, 400.0, 0.45, 0.08, 0.35, 450),
            (120.0, 2500.0, 0.22, 0.04, 0.45, 350),
        ]
    else:
        settings = [
            (5.0, 60.0, 0.65, 0.10, 0.25, 350),
            (20.0, 400.0, 0.40, 0.07, 0.35, 250),
            (120.0, 2500.0, 0.20, 0.03, 0.45, 200),
        ]

    grad_time = max(0.0, grad_end - time.monotonic())
    if grad_time > 10.0:
        per = max(8.0, grad_time / max(1, len(settings)))
        for s in settings:
            if time.monotonic() > grad_end - 5.0:
                break
            sub_dead = min(grad_end, time.monotonic() + per)
            seq, sc = _grad_polish(
                best,
                sub_dead,
                focus0=s[0],
                focus1=s[1],
                lr0=s[2],
                lr1=s[3],
                momentum=s[4],
                max_iters=s[5]
            )
            if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = seq.copy()
                best_sc = sc

    if do_lp and n <= 1600 and time.monotonic() < lp_end - 20.0:
        seq, sc = _lp_dir_optimize(
            best,
            lp_end,
            max_iters=max_lp_iters,
            timeout_max=lp_timeout
        )
        if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = seq.copy()
            best_sc = sc

    final_dead = deadline - 5.0
    if time.monotonic() < final_dead - 20.0:
        seq, sc = _grad_polish(
            best,
            final_dead,
            focus0=30.0,
            focus1=800.0,
            lr0=0.35,
            lr1=0.06,
            momentum=0.45,
            max_iters=250
        )
        if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = seq.copy()
            best_sc = sc

    if time.monotonic() < final_dead - 10.0:
        rngp = np.random.default_rng(seed + 1000 + n)
        seq, sc = _random_perturb_check(best, rngp, num=6, scales=(0.02, 0.05))
        if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = seq.copy()
            best_sc = sc

    sym, sym_sc = _try_symmetrize(best)
    if sym is not None and np.isfinite(sym_sc) and sym_sc < best_sc:
        best = sym.copy()
        best_sc = sym_sc

    print(f"[{name}] finish n={int(len(best))} score={best_sc:.6f}")
    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380.0, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        requested = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        requested = 2350.0
    if requested < 100.0:
        requested = 2350.0
    use_budget = min(2380.0, max(requested, 2350.0))
    deadline = t0 + use_budget

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    tasks = []

    # Use previous construction if present.
    try:
        gs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        gs = _finite_nonneg(gs)
        if gs is not None and gs.size >= 16:
            if gs.size > 4096:
                gs = gs[:4096]
            tasks.append((gs.copy(), "existing", 900.0, True))
            for L in (512, 1024):
                if gs.size >= L:
                    tasks.append((gs[:L].copy(), "existing_L%d" % L, 450.0, True))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Strong structural starts.
    tasks.append((_root_sequence(512), "root512", 850.0, True))
    tasks.append((_root_sequence(1024), "root1024", 650.0, True))

    for k in range(3):
        tasks.append((_modulated_root(512, rng, k), "mod512_%d" % k, 220.0, True))
    for k in range(2):
        tasks.append((_modulated_root(1024, rng, k), "mod1024_%d" % k, 220.0, True))

    tasks.append((_root_sequence(2048), "root2048", 300.0, False))

    for seq, name, alloc, use_lp in tasks:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 45.0:
            break
        now = time.monotonic()
        alloc = min(float(alloc), max(0.0, deadline - now - 45.0))
        if alloc < 45.0:
            continue

        n = int(len(seq))
        max_lp = 0
        if use_lp:
            if n <= 512:
                max_lp = 18
            elif n <= 1024:
                max_lp = 10
            else:
                max_lp = 4

        lp_timeout = 120.0 if n <= 512 else 90.0
        task_dead = time.monotonic() + alloc
        seq2, sc = _process_sequence(
            seq,
            task_dead,
            do_lp=(use_lp and n <= 1024),
            max_lp_iters=max_lp,
            lp_timeout=lp_timeout,
            name=name,
            seed=seed + 17 * len(name)
        )

        if seq2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_seq = np.asarray(seq2, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            best_score = float(sc)
            print(f"new best [{name}] score={best_score:.6f} n={len(best_seq)}")

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = _root_sequence(512)
        best_score = _score_sequence(best_seq)

    # Final polish, especially LP on the global best.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        n = int(len(best_seq))
        max_lp = 14 if n <= 512 else (8 if n <= 1024 else 3)
        seq2, sc = _process_sequence(
            best_seq,
            deadline,
            do_lp=(n <= 1024),
            max_lp_iters=max_lp,
            lp_timeout=150.0 if n <= 512 else 90.0,
            name="final",
            seed=seed + 12345
        )
        if seq2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_seq = np.asarray(seq2, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            best_score = float(sc)
            print(f"final new best score={best_score:.6f} n={len(best_seq)}")

    # Clean output.  Scaling is invariant; make max coefficient about 1.
    out_arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    out_arr = np.maximum(out_arr, 0.0)
    m = float(out_arr.max())
    if m > 0.0 and np.isfinite(m):
        out_arr = out_arr / m
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]

    try:
        ev = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        if np.isfinite(ev):
            print(f"final evaluate_sequence score={ev:.6f} n={len(out)}")
        else:
            fallback = _root_sequence(512)
            fallback = fallback / max(1e-300, float(fallback.max()))
            out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in fallback]
            print("evaluation failed, returning fallback root sequence")
    except Exception as e:
        print("evaluation unavailable:", e)

    return out
```
